- Semgrep p/kotlin/p/java/p/secrets rule packs are fetched from the registry at scan time even though the CLI is pinned to 1.169.0: the effective SAST policy is not reproducible from the repo alone.
- Semgrep records unparseable files in .errors[] without changing its exit code; a Kotlin file that fails to parse yields zero findings and still passes the gate (surfaced as a ::warning:: only).
- Accepted residual gap, stated in docs: secrets embedded only in the BUILT ARTIFACT (APK resources, strings.xml, assets, native libs) are not scanned today by anything.
